Evaluated across five diverse domains including Safety, Bias, and Ethics, MAESTRO outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by up to 10.61% in average performance retention under a strict 50% compression regime, while exhibiting substantially lower cross-task variance, indicating that global, routing-congruent pruning produces models that generalize more consistently across heterogeneous tasks.

</details>


### [111] [DominoTree: Conditional Tree-Structured Drafting with Domino for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08642)
*Saw S. Lin,Jyh-Shing Roger Jang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DominoTree 是一种训练无关的最佳优先树投机解码方法，利用 Domino 的条件化、非因子化修正对每条根到节点路径进行评分，通过将每节点修正限制在候选 top-M 实现实用性。在 Qwen3-4B/8B 上取得最高平均接受长度和吞吐量优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有块扩散drafter（如DFlash）仅建模逐位置边缘分布，而最佳优先树方法（如DDTree）从这些边缘分布构建候选树，但无法表示Domino的GRU因果修正所带来的路径依赖分布结构。需要一种能利用Domino条件化、非因子化修正的树结构解码方法。

Method: 提出 DominoTree：将 Domino 沿每条根到节点路径的条件化、非因子化修正作为树节点评分依据，通过将每节点修正限制在候选 top-M 使其计算可行。同时实现 GPU 原生的 CUDA-graph 树构建器，与 Python 参考实现比特一致，保证接受率不变且每轮构建开销低廉。

Result: 在 Qwen3-4B 八个基准上，DominoTree 相比自回归解码加速达 6.6 倍，平均接受长度最高达每轮 10.7 token，在所有测试温度下均最优。吞吐量比 Domino 解码器高 9-10%（Alpaca 上高达 +22%），在所有温度下均优于 DDTree/CaDDTree。在 Qwen3-8B 上保持最高接受长度，T=0 时吞吐量领先 DDTree +24%，高温下与 DDTree/CaDDTree 基本持平或略低，但整体仍优于 DFlash 和 Domino。

Conclusion: DominoTree 成功将 Domino 的路径依赖修正融入最佳优先树投机解码，在多个模型和温度设置下取得领先的接受长度和吞吐量，且树构建高效、接受率无损，证明了条件化非因子化修正对树结构解码的价值。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by drafting several tokens and verifying them in parallel. Block-diffusion drafters such as DFlash produce
  a draft block in one pass but model only per-position marginals; best-first tree methods such as DDTree expand candidate trees from those marginals. The
  released Domino drafter adds a GRU-based causal correction that makes each draft token's distribution path-dependent, a structure DDTree's factorized
  formulation cannot represent. We introduce DominoTree, a training-free best-first draft tree scored by Domino's conditional, non-factorized correction
  along each root-to-node path, made practical by restricting the per-node correction to a candidate top-M. On Qwen3-4B across eight benchmarks, DominoTree
  reaches up to 6.6x speedup over autoregressive decoding and the highest mean accept length of any evaluated method, up to 10.7 tokens per round, at every
  temperature we test. DominoTree constructs its tree with a GPU-native, CUDA-graph builder that is bit-identical to a reference Python implementation, so
  acceptance is unchanged, while keeping per-round tree construction cheap. With this builder as default, DominoTree wins throughput over the released
  Domino decoder at every temperature, 9-10% overall on Qwen3-4B and up to +22% on Alpaca, and over DDTree/CaDDTree at every temperature we test. On Qwen3-
  8B, DominoTree keeps the highest accepted length at every temperature and adds a decisive throughput win at T=0, +24% over DDTree; at higher temperature
  that edge over DDTree/CaDDTree narrows to a tie and a small loss, while its Overall aggregate wins over DFlash and Domino persist.

</details>


### [112] [UltraX: Refining Pre-Training Data at Scale with Adaptive Programmatic Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08646)
*Xinlong Zhao,Dongsheng Liu,Hengyu Zhao,Zixuan Fu,Zheng Wang,Jie Cai,Jie Zhou,Qiang Ma,Xuanhe Zhou,Xu Han,Yudong Wang,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UltraX 是一种面向大规模预训练数据的函数调用式精炼框架，通过在删除和修改之外引入插入操作，实现细粒度实例级编辑，并借助程序监督生成管道、低置信度过滤、比例控制采样及滑动窗口预测等机制，在提升数据质量的同时保证了效率与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着训练数据量逼近物理极限，Scaling Laws 的收益递减，提升 LLM 性能的关键从数据扩张转向数据质量利用。现有规则方法缺乏实例级灵活性，而 LLM 方法在效率与可靠性上无法满足大规模处理需求，亟需一种兼顾质量、效率与可靠性的精炼框架。

Method: UltraX 构建了一个函数调用式精炼框架：(1) 扩展编辑函数空间，新增插入操作，实现删除、修改、插入三类细粒度实例级编辑；(2) 通过数据集自适应提示优化引导专家 LLM 生成高质量端到端精炼文本，再利用行对齐映射与动态上下文替换将原文-精炼文对转化为结构化程序监督；(3) 采用低置信度样本过滤与基于操作组合的比例控制采样，提升监督质量并稳定训练分布；(4) 推理执行阶段通过滑动窗口预测、全局操作聚合及系统化后处理进行归一化与验证，增强大规模执行的稳定性与可靠性。

Result: 实验表明，UltraX 在所有语料上取得了最高平均性能，且在使用更少训练 token 的情况下达到或超越基线方法，展现出更强的数据效率与精炼可靠性。

Conclusion: UltraX 通过创新的函数调用式编辑框架及配套的监督生成与推理执行机制，在规模化数据精炼任务上实现了质量、效率与可靠性的统一，为后 Scaling Laws 时代的数据高效利用提供了有效路径。

Abstract: As available training data approaches its physical limit, gains from Scaling Laws have begun to diminish. Consequently, improving Large Language Models (LLMs) now depends less on data expansion and more on higher-quality data utilization. However, in the context of large-scale corpora, existing refinement methodologies face significant limitations in quality, efficiency, and reliability: Rule-based approaches are constrained by fixed heuristics and struggle with instance-level variations; LLM-based approaches improve quality but fail to meet the efficiency and reliability requirements of large-scale data processing. To address these challenges, we propose UltraX, a function-calling refinement framework for large-scale pre-training data that completes the editing function space by introducing insertion in addition to deletion and modification, enabling fine-grained instance-level editing. Specifically, UltraX builds a reliable program-supervision generation pipeline. In this pipeline, dataset-adaptive prompt optimization first guides an expert LLM to produce high-quality end-to-end refined texts, and Line Alignment Mapping and Dynamic Context Replacement then convert original-refined text pairs into structured program supervision. Meanwhile, UltraX improves supervision quality and stabilizes the training distribution with low-confidence example filtering and ratio-controlled sampling by operation combination. During inference and execution, it normalizes and validates model outputs through sliding-window prediction, global operation aggregation, and systematic post-processing, improving the stability and reliability of large-scale execution. Experiments show that UltraX achieves the highest average performance across all corpora and also matches or surpasses baselines with fewer training tokens, demonstrating stronger data efficiency and refinement reliability.

</details>


### [113] [WebSwarm: Recursive Multi-Agent Orchestration for Deep-and-Wide Web Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08662)
*Xiaoshuai Song,Liancheng Zhang,Kangzhi Zhao,Yutao Zhu,Zhongyuan Wang,Guanting Dong,Jinghan Yang,Han Li,Kun Gai,Ji-Rong Wen,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WebSwarm 是一种基于渐进式递归委托的多智能体网络搜索框架，通过动态实例化搜索节点、递归分解任务以及同级节点经验复用，在深度、广度和深度广度交织的搜索任务上一致优于单智能体和现有多智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单智能体 ReAct 式搜索受限于单一长轨迹和有限上下文，难以同时处理搜索深度和覆盖广度；多智能体系统虽通过并行执行提升覆盖率，但在递归深度、协作自适应性和基于证据的扩展方面仍存在明显不足。

Method: 提出 WebSwarm 框架，核心机制包括：(1) 渐进式递归委托，将任务分解、递归扩展和智能体协作在推理过程中联合构建；(2) 动态实例化搜索节点，每个节点耦合局部目标与搜索模式；(3) 节点可自行求解或委托子节点，完成后向上返回证据与结果供父节点扩展、修订或聚合；(4) 先探测网络上任务相关信息组织方式，以指导后续节点扩展；(5) 在同质兄弟节点间复用过程级经验。

Result: 在 BrowseComp-Plus、WideSearch、DeepWideSearch 和 GISA 四个基准上，WebSwarm 在深度、广度及深度广度交织任务中一致超越单智能体和多智能体基线。消融实验、任务难度分析、网络工具效率和模型泛化性分析进一步解释了其有效性。

Conclusion: WebSwarm 通过递归委托和证据驱动扩展有效解决了复杂网络搜索中深度与广度的权衡问题，为多智能体搜索系统的设计提供了新的思路和实践洞见。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based web search agents are transforming information seeking from simple factoid question answering into complex, deep-and-wide search and research-oriented tasks. A single ReAct-style agent is constrained by one long trajectory and limited context, making it difficult to handle depth and coverage simultaneously. Existing multi-agent systems improve search coverage through parallel execution and aggregation, but still exhibit clear limitations in recursive depth, collaboration adaptability, and evidence-grounded expansion. We propose WebSwarm, a progressive recursive delegation framework that jointly constructs task decomposition, recursive expansion, and agent collaboration during inference. WebSwarm dynamically instantiates agentic search nodes, each coupling a local objective with a search mode that specifies how the node should organize search and collaboration. Each node can either solve its objective itself or further delegate child nodes; after solving, it returns evidence and results upward, enabling parent nodes to further expand, revise, or aggregate the search process. To guide this process, WebSwarm first probes how task-relevant information is organized on the web to ground subsequent node expansion, and reuses process-level experience across homogeneous sibling nodes. Experiments on BrowseComp-Plus, WideSearch, DeepWideSearch, and GISA show that WebSwarm consistently outperforms single-agent and multi-agent baselines on deep, wide, and interleaved deep-and-wide tasks. Further analyses of ablation, task difficulty, web tool efficiency, and model generalization explain WebSwarm's effectiveness and provide insights for multi-agent search systems.

</details>


### [114] [Do You Need a Frontier Model as a Citation Verifier? Benchmarking Rubric LLMs for Deep-Research Source Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08700)
*Ethan Leung,Elias Lumer,Corey Feld,Austin Huber,Vamse Kumar Subbiah,Kevin Paul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在深度研究系统中，使用LLM作为评判者（judge）对引用质量进行评分时的校准问题。通过在对抗性长文本基准上对8个LLM评判者进行人工标注对比，发现廉价模型在源相关性和事实支持两个维度上均具竞争力，但F1分数掩盖了方向性偏差，校准评判者是将其用作强化学习奖励信号的前提。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习越来越依赖LLM评判者为每个评分标准打分并充当奖励模型。在将此类信号用于训练之前，必须了解评判者的能力下限及偏差程度。引用质量作为一个结构化评分任务，亟需对LLM评判者进行系统校准研究。

Method: 构建对抗性长文本基准，包含1,248条评分决策（全部经人工审核，其中378条为评判者分歧产生的困难案例）。评估来自3个模型家族的8个现成LLM评判者，在源相关性和事实支持两个维度上与金标准标签进行对比，采用F1、Cohen's κ、通过率漂移、假阳性率和假阴性率等指标。

Result: GPT-5-mini在源相关性上取得最高F1（0.908，κ=0.636）；在事实支持维度上所有评判者统计上不可区分（置信区间重叠）。在F1相当的情况下，评判者在通过率漂移、假阳性率和假阴性率上差异显著，标量F1掩盖了这种方向性偏差。

Conclusion: 校准LLM评判者是将其用作强化学习奖励信号的先决条件，因为方向性偏差会被下游RL循环放大。实验表明，这种校准并不需要最昂贵的模型，廉价模型同样胜任。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning increasingly relies on an LLM judge to score each rubric criterion, and that judge acts as the reward model during training. Before such a signal can be trusted, we need to know how capable the judge must be and how biased it is. We study this calibration question for citation quality in deep-research systems, where a search-grounded LLM must support each claim it writes with a cited source. Citation quality is a structured rubric task in which each attribution-citation pair is judged along two dimensions that require an LLM, source relevance and factual support. On an adversarial long-form benchmark, we score 8 off-the-shelf LLM judges from 3 model families against gold labels over 1,248 rubric decisions, all of which were human-reviewed and 378 of which were hard cases adjudicated from judge disagreements. Cheaper judges remain competitive across both dimensions, with GPT-5-mini attaining the strongest source-relevance pass-class F1 at 0.908 ($κ$=0.636), while on factual support the judges are statistically indistinguishable (overlapping confidence intervals), so no single model dominates. At comparable F1, the judges still differ substantially in pass-rate drift, false positive rate, and false negative rate. Scalar F1 obscures this directional bias, yet it is exactly what a downstream reinforcement learning loop would reinforce. Calibrating the judge is therefore a prerequisite for using citation rubrics as reward signals, and our results show that this calibration does not require the most expensive available model.

</details>


### [115] [Validity of LLMs as data annotators: AMALIA on authority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08731)
*Manuel Pita*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AMALIA，一个葡萄牙语国家语言模型，在道德基础编码任务上与人编码员一致性高，但通过"恢复缺口"测试发现其依赖表面相关性（如权威人物附近的道德愤怒）而非真正理解构念理论，表明一致性≠效度。研究建议国家模型评测应检验证据路径而非仅看一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国家语言模型在基准测试中与人类编码员的高一致性常被视作成功标志，但一致性和可靠性并不等同于有效性。对于需要推断的理论构念（如道德基础），模型可能通过表面捷径而非真正遵循构念理论来达成正确编码。这需要一种方法来区分模型是基于理论推理还是利用了相关捷径。

Method: 提出"恢复缺口"（recovery gap）测试：将整体提示分解为编码手册的原子条款，并通过理论的显式规则重新组合，测量性能损失。若校准能弥合该缺口，则某种可移植性应在模型和语言间幸存；否则构念-模型工具可能是失败源头。具体操作：将英语校准工具迁移至AMALIA-9B和欧洲葡萄牙语，在权威道德基础上测试，并进行错误分析。

Result: AMALIA在权威道德基础编码上F1仅比大8-13倍的开放模型低6个点。但分解仅恢复约一半的整体性能，错误分析显示其依赖表面相关，尤其是权威人物附近的道德愤怒。同一葡语语料上，开放多语言LLM在相同指令下弥合了该缺口，排除了语料为主要解释因素。

Conclusion: AMALIA可用于大规模筛选和预编码，但尚不能独立测量该构念。这是一个反例而非对国家模型的定论。主张主权LLM基准测试套件应同时测试模型与人类编码员的一致性，以及该一致性所依据的证据路径（即模型是否真正遵循构念理论）。

Abstract: A national language model offers a linguistic community its own instrument for measuring what its citizens say and value. Portugal's AMALIA, a publicly funded 9B-parameter model for European Portuguese, appears competitive on agreement alone: asked to code the moral foundation of authority, it agrees with trained human coders to within six F1 points of open models eight to thirteen times its size. Yet agreement is reliability, not validity. For theoretical constructs that must be inferred rather than read from surface features, the question is whether the model follows the construct's theory or reaches the right code by correlated shortcuts. We test this with the recovery gap: the loss in performance when a holistic prompt is decomposed into the codebook's atomic clauses and recombined by the theory's explicit rule. If calibration closes that gap, some portability should survive across models and languages; where it does not, the construct-model instrument is the likely locus of failure. We ask whether a calibrated English instrument transfers to AMALIA-9B and to European Portuguese. For one construct and one corpus, it does not. Decomposition recovers only about half of AMALIA's holistic performance, and error analysis suggests reliance on surface correlates, especially moral outrage near authority figures. An open multilingual LLM closes the gap on the same Portuguese corpus under the same instructions, pointing away from the corpus as the main explanation. AMALIA can still screen and pre-code at scale, but it cannot yet measure this construct well enough to stand alone. The study is a single counterexample, not a verdict on national models; it argues that sovereign-LLM benchmark batteries should test not only agreement with human coders, but the evidential route by which that agreement is warranted.

</details>


### [116] [UniClawBench: A Universal Benchmark for Proactive Agents on Real-World Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08768)
*Zhekai Chen,Chengqi Duan,Kaiyue Sun,Bohao Li,Yuqing Wang,Manyuan Zhang,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniClawBench 是首个能力驱动的基准测试，用于在动态真实环境中评估主动式智能体。围绕五项基础能力设计了400个双语任务，在Docker容器中进行逐步评估，并采用闭环评估策略模拟多轮人机反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试存在三大缺陷：(1)依赖沙盒环境和单轮评估范式，脱离真实场景；(2)基于场景的任务分类将多种模型能力混杂在同一类别中，难以定位智能体失败的根本原因；(3)静态预录答案无法反映动态交互过程。因此需要一个能力驱动、真实环境、多轮交互的评估框架。

Method: 提出UniClawBench基准，核心设计包括：(1)五项基础能力维度：技能使用、探索、长上下文推理、多模态理解和跨平台协调；(2)400个双语真实任务；(3)在实时Docker容器中评估，采用细粒度逐步完成检查点；(4)闭环评估策略：由执行者智能体、隐藏监督者智能体和用户智能体组成，模拟真实多轮反馈且不泄露评分标准；(5)在多种智能体框架下评估顶尖模型，解耦基础模型能力与框架设计的影响。

Result: 通过对多种模型和智能体框架的全面对比，揭示了基础模型能力与智能体框架设计如何共同塑造在真实环境中的性能表现。

Conclusion: UniClawBench 为主动式智能体的能力诊断提供了更细粒度的评估工具，有助于区分模型本身能力不足与框架设计缺陷，推动该领域的进一步研究。基准和代码已开源。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models and multimodal large language models has accelerated the emergence of proactive agents capable of operating everyday tools and assisting users in real-world environments. However, existing benchmarks struggle to evaluate such agents effectively, as they often rely on sandboxed environments and single-turn evaluation paradigms. Moreover, their scenario-based task taxonomies mix multiple model capabilities within the same task category, making it difficult to identify the root causes of agent failures. To address these limitations, we introduce UniClawBench, the first capability-driven benchmark designed to evaluate proactive agents in dynamic, real-world settings. UniClawBench is built around five foundational model capabilities: Skill Usage, Exploration, Long-Context Reasoning, Multimodal Understanding, and Cross-Platform Coordination. Based on these capabilities, we design 400 bilingual real-world tasks. Unlike previous benchmarks that rely on static, pre-recorded answers, our benchmark evaluates agents in live Docker containers using fine-grained, step-by-step completion checkpoints. Furthermore, we design a closed-loop evaluation strategy comprising an executor agent, a hidden supervisor agent, and a user agent to simulate realistic multi-turn human feedback without leaking grading criteria. To disentangle base model capabilities from framework-level design choices, we evaluate state-of-the-art models under multiple agent frameworks. Through comprehensive comparisons across both models and frameworks, we show how base model capabilities and agent framework designs jointly shape performance in real-world environments. To facilitate future research, we make our benchmark and code publicly available at https://github.com/HKU-MMLab/UniClawBench.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Mixtures of spatial factor analyzers for tensor-variate data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07887)
*Hanzhang Lu,Keiran Malott,Kirsty Milligan,Sanjeena Subedi,Edana Cassol,Vinita Chauhan,Connor McNairn,Prarthana Pasricha,Sangeeta Murugkar,Rowan Thomson,Andrew Jirasek,Jeffrey L. Andrews*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出混合空间因子分析器(MSFA)，利用样条空间衰减协方差结构和矩阵变量因子分析器解决高维空间数据聚类问题，通过EM算法与广义最小二乘进行参数估计，在张量数据分析中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维空间数据聚类面临维度灾难和参数膨胀问题，传统方法难以有效捕捉空间依赖关系，需要一种能够同时处理空间相关性和高维特征的降维聚类框架。

Method: 提出混合空间因子分析器(MSFA)：1) 利用样条基函数构建灵活的空间衰减协方差结构，随维度增加控制参数增长；2) 采用矩阵变量因子分析器处理非空间依赖性，实现进一步降维；3) 使用改进的EM算法结合广义最小二乘估计器进行参数推断。

Result: 模拟研究及在拉曼光谱和高光谱纹理数据库上的应用表明，MSFA能准确推断并区分不同的空间模式，在张量变量数据分析场景下表现出良好性能。

Conclusion: MSFA为高维空间数据聚类提供了一种有效的统一框架，通过样条空间衰减协方差与矩阵变量因子分析器的结合，成功克服了维度灾难，在空间模式识别任务中具有显著潜力。

Abstract: A mixture of spatial factor analyzers (MSFA) is introduced to address the challenges of clustering high-dimensional spatial data. By leveraging the underlying coordinate system, the proposed framework incorporates a flexible, spline-based spatial decay covariance structure that prevents parameter inflation as dimensionality increases. To model non-spatial dependence, matrix variate factor analyzers are employed for further dimensionality reduction. Parameter estimation is conducted via a variant of the expectation-maximization algorithm combined with a generalized least squares estimator. The proposed models are explored in the context of tensor-variate data analysis, where simulation studies and applications to Raman spectroscopy and hyperspectral texture databases demonstrate their capacity to accurately infer and differentiate distinct spatial patterns.

</details>


### [118] [Beta Regression with Autoregressive Errors for Interrupted Time Series Analysis of Proportion and Rate Outcomes: A Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07914)
*Ariel Linden*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了 betark，一种用于带有自回归误差的 beta 回归的 Stata 命令，采用联合条件最大似然估计。蒙特卡洛研究表明，相比使用 Newey-West HAC 标准误的拟二项 GLM，betark 在大多数场景下提供了更优校准的推断，尤其是在高度持续自相关的短序列情形下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中断时间序列分析（ITSA）中的比例和率结局具有有界性，但常用 OLS 回归处理。现有处理有界结局的方法（拟似然 GLM + HAC 标准误）无法联合建模 beta 分布条件密度与自回归误差结构，需要一种能够同时估计均值、精度和自回归系数的方法。

Method: 提出 betark：一种基于递归替换的联合条件最大似然估计器，可在单一似然函数中联合估计均值、精度和 AR(k) 系数，直接得出已考虑自相关的标准误。通过蒙特卡洛研究，在单组 ITSA 设计中比较 betark 与使用 Newey-West HAC 标准误的拟二项 GLM，涵盖 AR(1)–AR(3) 过程、三种序列长度和四种效应量。

Result: 两种方法基本无偏，但 betark 在多数场景下提供更优校准的推断，优势在高度持续自相关的短序列中最为显著——此时 GLM+HAC 的 I 类错误超过 60%。误设 AR 阶数（偏差一阶）以及改变起始均值和干预前趋势对性能影响不大。但在高度持续的 AR(3) 过程中，即使序列最长，betark 自身的 I 类错误仍然偏高。

Conclusion: betark 为 ITSA 中有界比例结局的建模提供了优于 GLM+HAC 的推断校准，尤其适用于自相关持续性较强的场景，但在高度持续高阶自相关下仍有 I 类错误膨胀的局限。

Abstract: Interrupted time series analyses (ITSA) of proportion and rate outcomes are frequently estimated using ordinary least squares regression despite the bounded nature of these outcomes. When methods appropriate for bounded outcomes are used, the standard approach is a quasi-likelihood generalized linear model (GLM) with heteroskedasticity- and autocorrelation-consistent (HAC) standard errors. However, no existing estimator jointly models the beta-distributed conditional density and autoregressive (AR) error structure. We introduce betark, a Stata implementation of a joint conditional maximum likelihood estimator for beta regression with AR(k) errors based on a recursive substitution that yields a closed-form conditional beta likelihood with autoregressive dependence of arbitrary order. Unlike two-stage approaches, betark jointly estimates the mean, precision, and AR(k) coefficients in a single likelihood, so reported standard errors directly account for autocorrelation without separate correction. A Monte Carlo study compared betark with a quasi-binomial GLM using Newey-West HAC standard errors across AR(1)-AR(3) processes, three series lengths, and four effect sizes in a single-group ITSA design. Both methods were essentially unbiased, but betark produced better-calibrated inference than GLM+HAC in most scenarios, with the largest gains under highly persistent autocorrelation, where GLM+HAC Type I error exceeded 60% for short series. Misspecifying the AR order by one lag and varying the starting mean and pre-intervention trend had only modest effects on performance. However, betark's own Type I error remained elevated under highly persistent AR(3) processes even for the longest series examined.

</details>


### [119] [Size independence of consistency index for pairwise comparison matrices in analytic hierarchy process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07950)
*Tsuneshi Obata,Shunsuke Shiraishi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究了层次分析法中成对比较矩阵的一致性指标，重点探讨了矩阵规模对一致性评估的影响，提出了一个与矩阵规模无关的一致性指标应满足的基本性质，并对作者此前提出的指标进行了改进，使其与现有指标相一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Saaty提出的CI和CR一致性指标采用固定阈值0.1作为可接受一致性水平的准则，但该阈值是否在不同矩阵规模（即不同评价项数量）下代表同等一致性水平尚不明确。这一问题驱动作者探究一致性与矩阵规模之间的关系。

Method: 通过从评价项子集构建成对比较矩阵，分析一致性与矩阵规模之间的关系；提出与规模无关的一致性指标应满足的基本性质；对作者先前基于特征多项式系数与一致性关系提出的指标进行改进以满足该性质；利用随机生成的成对比较矩阵对矩阵规模与一致性指标值的关系进行可视化分析。

Result: 提出了与规模无关的一致性指标应满足的基本性质；改进后的指标被证明与现有一致性指标一致；通过可视化揭示了矩阵规模对一致性评估的具体影响。

Conclusion: 矩阵规模对一致性评估存在显著影响，Saaty的固定阈值0.1在不同矩阵规模下可能不等价。所提出的规模无关性质及改进指标为更合理的一致性评估提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Pairwise comparisons are fundamental in the analytic hierarchy process. Various consistency indices have been proposed to assess inconsistencies in these comparisons. Since Saaty first proposed his consistency index, the assessment of the degree of consistency in pairwise comparison matrices has remained an open and hot topic in the study of the analytic hierarchy process. The consistency indices CI and CR proposed by Saaty are defined using the principal eigenvalue of the pairwise comparison matrix. In our previous study, we introduced an alternative index derived from the relationship between the coefficient of the characteristic polynomial and the consistency of comparisons.
  Saaty proposed a fixed threshold of 0.1 for CI or CR as a guideline for an acceptable level of consistency, regardless of the matrix size. However, whether this threshold represents an equivalent level of consistency across different matrix sizes, that is, across different numbers of evaluation items, remains unclear. This study analysed the relationship between consistency and matrix size by examining pairwise comparison matrices constructed from subsets of evaluation items. Based on this analysis, we propose the fundamental property to be satisfied by a size-independent consistency index.
  Furthermore, we refine our previously proposed index to ensure that it satisfies this property, demonstrating that it coincides with the existing consistency index. Finally, we visualise the relationship between the matrix size and consistency index values using randomly generated pairwise comparison matrices, thereby providing insights into the impact of matrix size on consistency evaluation.

</details>


### [120] [Evaluation of Combination Therapy amid Patient-Level Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08058)
*Lingjie Wei,Xinzhou Guo,Shuoxun Xu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 联合疗法获益可能源自患者对单一疗法的异质性响应，而非药理学相互作用。本文提出一种无模型统计推断框架，通过调整患者层面的异质性来评估联合疗法，解决了不可识别性和非线性问题，并利用基于结局的最优匹配方法构建置信区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合疗法的临床获益传统上被归因于药物间的药理学相互作用，但近期药理学实验表明，部分联合疗法的优势可能主要源于患者对单一疗法的异质性响应。经典统计推断框架未考虑这种患者层面的异质性，可能导致评估结果不充分且过于乐观。因此需要一个新的统计框架来正确评估联合疗法。

Method: 提出一种无模型的统计推断框架：1）定义跨世界目标参数（cross-world target parameter）以捕捉联合疗法超越患者异质性响应的真实增益；2）解决调整患者异质性时固有的非可识别性和非线性问题，建立目标参数（部分）可识别性条件；3）开发基于结局的最优匹配方案（outcome-based optimal matching），实现渐近正态性并构建√N速率的置信区间。

Result: 所提方法能够可靠、高效且透明地评估联合疗法在患者异质性背景下的真实疗效。通过对ACTG 175试验的再分析验证了该框架的实际效用。

Conclusion: 在评估联合疗法时，必须考虑并调整患者对单一疗法的异质性响应，否则将高估联合疗法的药理学协同效应。该框架为联合疗法的统计评估提供了更严谨的无模型替代方案。

Abstract: Combination therapy, a treatment approach that involves two or more monotherapies, is widely considered to enhance therapeutic efficacy across different medical conditions. It was often believed that combination therapy is beneficial because of pharmacological interactions between its component monotherapies. However, through laboratory experiments, pharmacologists have recently noted that the benefits of some combination therapies might be largely driven by varying patient-level responses to their component monotherapies. Without accounting for such patient-level heterogeneity, classical statistical inference frameworks for combination therapy might be inadequate and overly optimistic. In this paper, we introduce a novel and model-free statistical inference framework to complement the classical one and evaluate combination therapy after adjusting for patient-level heterogeneity in responses to monotherapies. We address the non-identifiability and nonlinearity issue inherent in adjustment of patient-level heterogeneity and establish conditions for the (partial) identifiability of the cross-world target parameter. We develop an outcome-based optimal matching scheme to achieve asymptotic normality and construct $\sqrt{N}$-rate confidence intervals for the target parameter, thereby enabling reliable, efficient and transparent evaluation of combination therapy amid patient-level heterogeneity. The benefits of the proposed framework are demonstrated through a reanalysis of the ACTG 175 trial.

</details>


### [121] [Bias-Corrected Multiplier Bootstrap Inference for Spectral Edges of Large Covariance Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08089)
*Xiucai Ding,Yichen Hu,Jiahui Xie*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对高维协方差矩阵谱边缘推断中Tracy-Widom尺度波动的估计难题，本文提出一种偏差校正的乘数自助法(bias-corrected multiplier bootstrap)，通过精心校准的乘数扰动将边缘波动正则化到稍大的尺度，使得高斯近似可行，从而构造出有效的置信区间，并衍生出一个无阈值的尖峰数量估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在一般未知总体协方差结构下，最大非尖峰样本特征值作为谱边缘的自然估计量，其波动服从Tracy-Widom分布。有效的统计推断需要精确的中心化(确定谱边缘)和精确的尺度常数，但在实践中两者都难以估计。因此需要一种在弱假设下仍有效的谱边缘推断方法。

Method: 提出偏差校正的乘数自助法(bias-corrected multiplier bootstrap)：引入精心校准的乘数扰动，将边缘波动正则化到稍大的尺度，使高斯近似变得可处理；通过自助特征值直接构造置信区间，并结合数据驱动的重新中心化步骤校正自助法引起的确定性边缘偏移；经偏差校正和重新缩放后，证明最大的几个非尖峰自助特征值条件渐近服从高斯分布。

Result: (1) 偏差校正和重新缩放后，最大非尖峰自助特征值在给定数据下渐近高斯；(2) 所提置信区间渐近有效，长度仅略大于Tracy-Widom尺度；(3) 在备择假设下(附加尖峰以大于n^{-1/6}的局部尺度从主体分离)，覆盖率趋于零；(4) 同一置信区间可给出尖峰数量的无阈值估计，无需尖峰互异或非常大，等价于为碎石图提供数据驱动且理论合理的截断准则。

Conclusion: 所提出的偏差校正乘数自助法为高维协方差矩阵谱边缘推断提供了一种实用且理论可靠的解决方案，既能构造有效的置信区间，又能同时实现尖峰数量的自适应估计，为碎石图提供了首个数据驱动且理论保证的截断方法。

Abstract: Inference for spectral edges of large covariance matrices is a fundamental problem in high-dimensional statistics. A major difficulty is that the largest non-spiked sample eigenvalues, which serve as natural estimators of the edge, fluctuate on the Tracy--Widom scale. Consequently, valid inference requires accurate centering by the deterministic spectral edge together with a precise scaling constant, both of which are often difficult to estimate in practice under general unknown population covariance structures. In this paper, we propose a bias-corrected multiplier bootstrap procedure for inference on the deterministic edge of the bulk spectrum. The key idea is to introduce a carefully calibrated multiplier perturbation that regularizes the edge fluctuation to a slightly larger scale at which Gaussian approximation becomes tractable. The resulting confidence interval is constructed directly from bootstrap eigenvalues, together with a data-driven recentering step that corrects the bootstrap-induced shift of the deterministic edge. On the theoretical side, we show that, after bias correction and rescaling, the largest few non-spiked bootstrap eigenvalues are asymptotically Gaussian conditionally on the data. Building on this result, we establish the asymptotic validity of the proposed confidence interval, whose length is only slightly larger than the Tracy--Widom scale, and prove vanishing coverage under alternatives in which additional spikes separate from the bulk at a local scale larger than $n^{-1/6}$. As a consequence, the same confidence interval yields a threshold-free estimator for the number of spikes, without requiring the spikes to be distinct or very large. Equivalently, the procedure yields a data-driven and theoretically justified cutoff for the scree plot.

</details>


### [122] [Joint estimation of high-dimensional spiked covariance matrices via a partially shared subspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08123)
*Changwon Yoon,Minwoo Kim,Sungkyu Jung,Jeongyoun Ahn*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于尖峰协方差模型（spiked covariance model）的框架，用于两数据集间共享部分协方差结构（共享一个未知秩和频谱位置的子空间）的联合估计，包括共享子空间与秩的估计、封闭形式的池化权重，并提供了比例增长机制下的渐近保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据分析常受限于小样本量，但来自相关来源的辅助数据集往往可用。当两个数据集共享部分（而非全部）协方差结构时，利用共享部分可显著改善估计效率。现有模型未能充分捕捉这种"部分共享"的协方差结构。

Method: 提出对称处理两数据集的尖峰协方差模型，两数据集共享一个未知秩及频谱中任意位置的子空间方向，同时各自保留独有的尖峰方向。开发了完整的联合估计流程：共享子空间及其秩的联合估计、结合两数据集的封闭形式池化权重，并基于随机矩阵理论在比例增长机制（proportional-growth regime）下推导渐近保证。

Result: 该框架推广了现有共享协方差结构模型，填补了对比降维（contrastive dimension reduction）在高维情境下的估计缺口，并在COVID-19早期投资组合构建和脑肿瘤基因表达的对比分析两个应用场景中得到验证。

Conclusion: 模型为两数据集间部分共享协方差结构提供了严格且实用的估计框架，兼具理论保证与实证效果。

Abstract: Statistical analysis of high-dimensional data is often hampered by limited sample sizes, yet auxiliary datasets from related sources are often readily available. When two such datasets share part of their covariance structure, but not all of it, exploiting the shared part can substantially improve estimation. We propose a spiked covariance model that explicitly captures this partial sharing: two datasets share a subspace of unknown rank and arbitrary position in the spectrum, while each retains its own distinct spiked directions. The model treats the two datasets symmetrically and strictly generalizes existing models for shared covariance structure. We develop a complete estimation procedure that includes joint estimation of the shared subspace and its rank, a closed-form pooling weight for combining the two datasets, and asymptotic guarantees derived from random matrix theory in the proportional-growth regime. The framework also resolves a gap in contrastive dimension reduction by providing a principled estimator for high-dimensional settings. We illustrate the methodology on portfolio construction during the early COVID-19 pandemic and on contrastive analysis of brain tumor gene expression.

</details>


### [123] [Bayesian Donor Set Selection in Synthetic Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08142)
*Seul Lee,Johan Lim,Joungyoun Kim,Xinlei Wang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种贝叶斯层次模型，在保持标准SCM单纯形约束的同时进行供体集选择，通过分层Gamma-Bernoulli构造实现对不相关供体的精确零权重，并在理论上证明了后验供体集一致性，在数值模拟和西德GDP案例中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统合成控制方法(SCM)的效果高度依赖于供体集的精心选择，但现有方法大多假设供体集固定，缺乏在模型内部自动筛选供体的机制，导致当供体池包含不相关或弱相关单元时估计效果下降。

Method: 提出一个贝叶斯层次模型：(1) 采用分层Gamma-Bernoulli先验构造供体权重，使得权重可以落在单纯形的面上，即允许将不相关供体的权重精确压缩为零；(2) 同时估计合成控制权重和活跃供体集，实现供体选择与权重估计的一体化；(3) 在简化干预前模型下建立了后验供体集一致性理论结果。

Result: 数值模拟表明：当供体池包含不相关或弱相关单元时，该模型在供体恢复和权重估计方面均优于传统方法；在全供体设定下仍保持竞争力。西德GDP轨迹的实证应用展示了其实际可操作性。

Conclusion: 将供体集选择纳入贝叶斯合成控制框架，为现有方法提供了一个更简约、更灵活的扩展方案。

Abstract: The Synthetic Control Method (SCM) is a widely used approach for assessing the effects of interventions by constructing a synthetic counterfactual using a donor set of untreated units. However, the effectiveness of SCM heavily relies on the careful selection of an appropriate donor set. In this paper, we propose a Bayesian hierarchical model that performs donor set selection while preserving the standard SCM simplex constraint on donor weights. Unlike approaches that assume a fixed donor set, our model allows for the simultaneous estimation of the synthetic control weights and the active donor set. By using a hierarchical Gamma-Bernoulli construction for the donor weights, the proposed model assigns posterior mass to simplex faces and allows exact zero weights for excluded donors. We establish a posterior donor-set consistency result under a simplified pre-intervention model. Through numerical simulations, we show that our model improves donor recovery and weight estimation when the donor pool contains irrelevant or weakly related units, while remaining competitive in full-donor settings. Finally, we apply our model to the GDP trajectory of West Germany, illustrating its practical applicability. Our findings suggest that incorporating donor set selection offers a more parsimonious and flexible extension of existing Bayesian synthetic control methods.

</details>


### [124] [Circular Expectiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08306)
*Bernhard Klar*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文引入循环期望分位数（circular expectiles），作为基于弦距离的非对称循环损失函数的极小化元。作者证明了在具有正密度的圆分布下该极小化元的存在唯一性，定义了经验循环期望分位数，并证明了其相合性和渐近正态性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将线性期望分位数（expectile）概念推广到方向数据领域。传统的线性期望分位数基于欧氏距离，无法直接适用于圆上的方向数据。需要构造一个适合循环数据的非对称损失函数，并解决由此产生的非凸优化问题。

Method: 1) 基于弦距离（chord distance）构造非对称循环损失函数；2) 以样本循环均值作为参考方向，在圆上定义线性序，从而定义经验循环期望分位数；3) 分析目标函数和识别函数的性质，给出几何解释；4) 证明存在性、唯一性、相合性及有限维渐近正态性。

Result: 1) 循环期望分位数保留了循环均值作为 α=1/2 对称情形的特例；2) 在具有正密度的圆分布下，极小化元存在且唯一；3) 经验循环期望分位数具有唯一性、相合性和有限维渐近正态性。

Conclusion: 循环期望分位数成功将线性期望分位数推广至方向数据，为循环数据的离散度、偏度和对称性诊断等应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce circular expectiles as minimizers of an asymmetric circular loss function based on chord distance. In contrast to the linear expectile criterion, the resulting circular optimization problem is non-convex, so existence and uniqueness require a separate analysis. The construction extends linear expectiles to directional data while preserving the circular mean as the symmetric case corresponding to $α=1/2$. We derive basic representations of the objective function and the associated identification function, and give a geometric interpretation that generalizes the corresponding representation for the circular mean. Furthermore, we prove the existence and uniqueness of the minimizers for distributions with positive density on the circle. The empirical circular expectile is defined by using the sample circular mean as reference direction for the induced linear order on the circle. We prove the uniqueness of the empirical expectile, as well as its consistency and finite-dimensional asymptotic normality. Finally, we indicate possible applications to circular measures of dispersion, skewness, and symmetry diagnostics.

</details>


### [125] [A scalable version of MADD for big-data classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08334)
*Annesha Ghosh,Adrija Saha,Soham Sarkar*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对高维低样本量场景中MADD距离度量计算复杂度过高的问题，本文提出了一种可扩展的MADD版本，通过选取代表性样本集和利用随机傅里叶特征，大幅降低计算复杂度，同时保持与原始MADD相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧氏距离在高维数据中受距离集中效应、邻域结构破坏和hub节点等问题困扰。MADD作为一种数据驱动的半度量能有效规避这些问题，但其计算复杂度随训练样本量呈二次增长，导致无法应用于大样本高维数据集。

Method: 提出可扩展MADD：1）在计算MADD时选取代表性样本集来替代全量样本，降低计算量；2）引入随机傅里叶特征（Random Fourier Features）进一步加速，尤其适用于超大样本场景。从理论和数值实验两方面验证了所提方法能在极低的计算时间成本下保持与原始MADD相近的性能。

Result: 所提方法在性能上与原始MADD相当，但计算时间仅为原始方法的一小部分，理论上和数值实验均得到了验证。

Conclusion: 该方法拓宽了MADD的适用范围，使其能够应用于具有大量观测样本的大数据场景。

Abstract: Distance-based classifiers are very popular, and the Euclidean distance is one of the most commonly used metrics in distance-based classifiers. However, classifiers based on the Euclidean distance often suffer in high-dimensional setups due to issues such as distance concentration, violation of neighborhood structures, and the presence of hubs. In high-dimension, low-sample-size (HDLSS) situations, a data-driven semi-metric called the Mean Absolute Difference of Distances (MADD) is known to circumvent these issues. But one major problem with MADD is that its computational complexity increases quadratically with the training sample size. As a result, the application of MADD becomes computationally challenging for big datasets that have both a high dimension as well as a large number of observations. In this paper, we propose a scalable version of MADD that significantly reduces its computational complexity while retaining its advantages. This speed-up is achieved by selecting a representative set during the computation of MADD. Further speed-ups are achieved by using the idea of Random Fourier Features, particularly when the sample size is very large. We establish that our proposed methods achieve performances similar to MADD but only at a fraction of its computing time, both theoretically as well as numerically. Our approach broadens the scope of MADD, allowing its use to big-data with a very large number of observations.

</details>


### [126] [Testing Covariance Separability in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08388)
*Tomas Masak,Marcus Mayrhofer,Una Radojičić*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在高维设定下检验矩阵变量数据协方差可分离性的方法，将其转化为白化后的球形检验，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟校准。同时引入基于径向归一化的角度版本来减少分布假设依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵变量数据分析中，协方差的可分离性是一个重要的结构假设，能极大简化协方差相关统计任务的解释和计算。然而在高维情形下，检验可分离性假设十分困难，现有方法存在局限性。

Method: 作者将可分离性检验问题重新构造为球形检验：首先使用可分离最大似然估计对数据进行白化，然后对白化后的协方差进行球形检验。检验通过蒙特卡洛模拟进行校准以保证有限样本下的水平控制。为进一步减少对分布假设的依赖，引入基于白化后径向归一化的"角度版本"检验。

Result: 理论层面证明了该检验在稠密备择假设下的高维一致性；数值实验展示了该方法在实际应用中的有效性、经验功效和计算效率。

Conclusion: 所提方法为高维矩阵变量数据的协方差可分离性检验提供了一种实用、高效且理论上可靠的解决方案，角度版本进一步增强了其对分布假设的稳健性。

Abstract: Separability is an important structural assumption often placed on the covariance when working with matrix-variate data, because it greatly simplifies both interpretation and computation of subsequent covariance-based statistical tasks. Yet testing the separability assumption is difficult in the high-dimensional regime. We propose to test separability by recasting the problem as a sphericity test after whitening the data using the separable maximum likelihood estimate of the covariance. The test is calibrated by Monte Carlo simulation, yielding finite-sample level control. Furthermore, we prove the test's high-dimensional consistency under dense alternatives. To reduce its reliance on distributional assumptions, we introduce an angular version of the test based on radial normalization after whitening. We demonstrate the practical utility, empirical power, and computational efficiency of the prop

</details>


### [127] [Bayesian DAG Structure Learning with Simultaneous Shrinkage Covariance Estimation under Scale-Mixture Error Distributions in the Proportional High-Dimensional Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08428)
*Samaneh Nazari,Mohammad Arashi,Abdolnasser Sadeghkhani*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出R-DACH框架，在高维比例渐近区域下，通过在马氏链分解的Cholesky因子下三角元素上放置horseshoe先验，实现联合DAG结构学习与精度矩阵估计，并通过观测级逆伽马混合实现鲁棒性，配合部分塌缩Gibbs采样器实现推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维情形下（p/n→c>0），现有DAG结构学习方法对重尾分布和污染观测敏感，缺乏统一的贝叶斯框架同时处理结构学习、精度矩阵估计和鲁棒性建模。

Method: 在DAG-Markov精度矩阵的修正Cholesky因子的严格下三角元素上直接放置global-local horseshoe型先验；每观测引入逆伽马尺度混合以实现对重尾和污染的自动鲁棒性；设计部分塌缩的分块Gibbs采样器联合遍历排序、稀疏模式和连续参数空间。

Result: 在p达数百的多种(n,p)配置仿真中验证了理论收敛速率，在污染数据下相比graphical-horseshoe、DAG-Wishart和PC算法有显著增益。TCGA RNA-seq基因表达数据应用中揭示了生物学可解释的调控结构，竞争方法未能恢复。

Conclusion: R-DACH为高维DAG结构学习提供了统一、鲁棒且计算可行的贝叶斯方案，同时实现排序推断、稀疏图选择和精度矩阵估计，在理论和应用上均表现优越。

Abstract: We propose a unified Bayesian framework namely robust DAG-Cholesky horseshoe (R-DACH) for joint directed acyclic graph (DAG) structure learning and precision matrix estimation in the high-dimensional proportional asymptotic regime $p/n \to c \in (0,\infty)$, under the scale mixture of normal errors. The construction places a global-local horseshoe-type prior directly on the strictly lower-triangular entries of the modified Cholesky factor of the DAG-Markov precision matrix, so that sparsity in the Cholesky parameters induces a coherent parent-set selection consistent with a topological ordering of the variables. A per-observation inverse-gamma scale mixture yields automatic robustness to heavy-tailed and contaminated observations and admits Student-$t$, Laplace, and slash distributions as special cases. We design a partially-collapsed blocked Gibbs sampler that traverses the joint space of orderings, sparsity patterns and continuous parameters. Simulations across $(n,p)$ configurations with $p$ up to several hundreds confirm the theoretical rates and demonstrate substantial gains over graphical-horseshoe, DAG-Wishart, and PC-based competitors under contamination. An application to RNA-seq gene-expression data from \emph{The Cancer Genome Atlas} reveals biologically interpretable regulatory structure that competing methods fail to recover.

</details>


### [128] [weightflow: declarative, recipe-aware survey weighting in R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08491)
*Juan Pablo Ferreira*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: weightflow 是一个基于 R 语言的依赖无关包，通过声明式、可管道化的 API 将复杂调查权重的整个层级调整流程（从资格判定、无应答校正到校准）封装为单一可审计对象，并通过重抽样自助法和刀切法将每一阶段的不确定性传播到重复权重中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 R 工具仅覆盖复杂调查权重工作流的部分环节，无法将整个级联流程表达为单一可审计对象，亦无法将每一调整阶段的可变性传播到重复权重中，导致方差估计未能完整反映所有估计步骤的不确定性。

Method: 提出 weightflow 包：采用 tidymodels 风格的声明式 API，将权重调整链定义为 step_*() 惰性配方，通过 prep() 统一估计；分离定义与应用使得重抽样自助法和"删除一个初级抽样单元"刀切法可在每个重复样本上重新执行整个配方，从而实现全阶段不确定性传播；实现了 raking、后分层、线性/GREG 校准（含界限、岭惩罚、域分区及整合变体）、模型辅助校准、加权组及机器学习响应倾向调整（含交叉拟合）以及代表性指标。

Result: 与 survey 包对比验证了校准和方差结果的一致性；在捆绑样本和乌拉圭 ECH 真实住户调查微数据上演示了完整级联流程，成功恢复了已知贫困率并给出基于设计的标准误。

Conclusion: weightflow 填补了调查权重工作流的缺失环节，通过全阶段不确定性传播和可审计的声明式配方，为复杂调查分析提供了更完整、可复现的权重构建方案，并与现有 survey 生态无缝桥接。

Abstract: Producing analysis weights for a complex survey requires a sequence of hierarchical adjustments (resolving unknown eligibility, dropping out-of-scope units, restoring within-household selection, correcting for nonresponse, and calibrating to known population totals), after which design-consistent variances must account for the fact that several adjustments were themselves estimated from the sample. Existing R tools cover parts of this workflow, but none expresses the whole cascade as a single auditable object, nor propagates the variability of every stage into the replicate weights. We present weightflow, a dependency-free (base R) package that builds survey weights through a declarative, pipeable, tidymodels-style API: a recipe is defined lazily as a chain of step_*() adjustments and estimated with prep(). Separating definition from application makes the process reproducible and auditable, and lets a rescaling bootstrap and a delete-a-PSU jackknife re-apply the entire recipe on each replicate, so the replicate weights carry the uncertainty of every estimated stage, not only of the final calibration. The package implements raking, post-stratification and linear/GREG calibration (with bounds, ridge penalisation, and domain-partitioned and integrative variants), model-assisted (Wu-Sitter) calibration, weighting-class and machine-learning response-propensity adjustments with cross-fitting, and representativity (R-)indicators. Weights and replicate weights bridge to the survey and srvyr packages for design-based inference. We validate the calibration and variance results against survey, and illustrate the full cascade on a bundled sample and on real household-survey microdata (the Uruguayan ECH), where it recovers a known poverty rate with design-based uncertainty.

</details>


### [129] [Calibrated Persistent-Laplacian CUSUM for Online Change-Point Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08635)
*Shiheng Nie,Yunguang Yue*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出PL-CUSUM，一种基于持续拉普拉斯谱的在线变点检测方法，将滑动窗口转为点云，用Page-CUSUM递归进行监控，理论上分析了误报控制与检测延迟，实验证明方法有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于持续同调摘要的检测器仅利用拓扑计数信息（如贝蒂数），忽略了谱信息中蕴含的尺度内连通性和几何结构。作者希望充分利用持续拉普拉斯算子的谱信息，提升高维非线性时间序列在线变点检测的性能。

Method: 1）将滑动窗口数据转换为点云；2）计算持续拉普拉斯谱，构造监控得分；3）将该得分输入Page-CUSUM递归框架进行在线检测；4）理论推导oracle检测器的误报-延迟界，并证明plug-in白化得分在有限监控期内控制误报；5）设计Phase I/Phase II流程：Phase I进行参数选择和阈值校准，Phase II执行在线递归检测。

Result: 理论上推导了oracle检测器的误报-延迟界，并证明plug-in白化得分在有限监控期内仍能控制误报。仿真系统和真实监控数据的实验表明，PL-CUSUM具有稳定的误报控制和有竞争力的检测性能。

Conclusion: PL-CUSUM通过引入持续拉普拉斯谱信息，相比仅依赖持续同调摘要的方法，能够更全面地捕获点云的多尺度拓扑与几何结构，在保持误报控制的同时实现高效的在线变点检测。

Abstract: We propose the Persistent Laplacian Cumulative Sum (PL-CUSUM), an online change-point detection method for high-dimensional nonlinear time series. The method converts sliding windows into point clouds and uses persistent Laplacian spectra to construct the monitoring score for the Page cumulative sum (Page-CUSUM) recursion. Compared with detectors based only on persistent-homology summaries, PL-CUSUM further uses spectral information to capture within-scale connectivity and geometric structure beyond homology counts. Theoretically, we analyze two key performance criteria: false-alarm control and detection delay. We derive false-alarm-delay bounds for the oracle detector and show that the plug-in whitened score still controls false alarms over a finite monitoring horizon. Methodologically, we provide a Phase I/Phase II procedure that performs parameter selection and control-limit calibration before online recursion. Experiments on simulated systems and real monitoring data show that PL-CUSUM provides stable false-alarm control and competitive detection performance.

</details>


### [130] [Severity estimation in dependent collective risk models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08719)
*Christopher Blier-Wong*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 该论文指出，当索赔次数与索赔金额存在相依性时，直接对池化后的索赔数据拟合边缘分布是不一致的；作者推导了池化索赔的真实分布（即任意观测索赔的分布，为条件分布的规模偏倚混合），并基于此构建了复合似然估计方法，在保单层面建立渐近性质，最后通过Sarmanov模型进行模拟验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在集体风险模型中，索赔次数与索赔金额（severity）通常被假设独立，但在实际保险数据中二者往往存在相依性。当相依性存在时，池化后的索赔数据不再是对金额边缘分布的随机抽样，直接拟合会导致不一致估计。作者旨在识别池化索赔所服从的真实分布，并据此构建一致且有效的估计方法。

Method: 首先从理论上证明，池化索赔的经验分布收敛到任意观测索赔的分布，该分布是条件索赔金额分布的规模偏倚（size-biased）混合。基于这一识别结果，作者构建了复合似然（composite likelihood）估计方法，以保单而非索赔为抽样单元，并推导了估计量的一致性和渐近正态性，信息矩阵采用Godambe信息。在Sarmanov集体风险模型框架下，导出了观测索赔密度和聚合均值的闭合形式，便于实际应用。

Result: 理论结果：池化索赔的真实分布是规模偏倚的条件分布混合；基于此的复合似然估计量具有一致性和渐近正态性，标准误应以保单为单元计算。模拟结果：展示了朴素池化拟合的偏差程度、复合似然方法对该偏差的修正效果，以及保单层面标准误的覆盖概率表现。

Conclusion: 当索赔次数与金额存在相依性时，传统直接拟合池化索赔边缘分布的方法是不一致的。应采用本文提出的复合似然方法，以保单为抽样单元，在观测索赔分布上进行估计，这样才能获得一致且推断有效的参数估计。

Abstract: The collective risk model represents the aggregate loss of an insurance portfolio as a random sum of individual claim severities. When claim counts and severities are dependent, the claims pooled across policies are no longer a sample from the marginal severity distribution. We show that their empirical distribution converges to the law of an arbitrary observed claim, a size-biased mixture of the conditional severity distributions, so any procedure that fits the severity margin directly to pooled claims is inconsistent in general. The same result identifies the distribution that the pooled claims do sample, and we build a composite likelihood estimation procedure on that distribution. We establish consistency and asymptotic normality, with Godambe information in which the policy, rather than the claim, is the sampling unit. In a Sarmanov collective risk model, the observed-claim density and the aggregate mean are in closed form. A simulation study measures the bias of naive pooled-severity fitting, its correction by the composite likelihood, and the coverage of the policy-level standard errors.

</details>


### [131] [On improving the estimates of the sampling variances via Global-Local priors in Small Area Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08720)
*Sirapat Watakajaturaphon,Jairo Fúquene-Patiño*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对Fay-Herriot模型中将抽样方差视为已知的局限，提出一种新的贝叶斯方法，利用Global-Local先验借助广义方差函数(GVF)稳定并改进小域估计中抽样方差的后验估计，并通过理论和模拟验证其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典FH模型假设抽样方差已知，但实际中抽样方差需从数据估计且往往波动较大。虽然广义方差函数(GVF)可借力跨区域稳定估计，但其平滑可能过度或不足。本文旨在通过贝叶斯框架下的Global-Local先验，在GVF基础上实现更灵活、更精准的收缩，改善抽样方差的后验估计质量。

Method: 提出一种分层贝叶斯模型，在GVF提供的基准估计之上引入Global-Local(GL)先验（如马蹄先验、拉普拉斯先验等），对抽样方差实施自适应收缩。分析了模型的理论性质，并开发了自适应MCMC算法以解决涉及Gamma函数条件分布带来的计算难题。

Result: 模拟研究表明，所提方法相比竞争方法在抽样方差估计和小域均值估计上均有更优表现。在美国农业部玉米产量估计和哥伦比亚市镇级教育成就指数两个实际应用中，该方法也展现出良好的估计精度和稳定性。

Conclusion: 基于Global-Local先验的贝叶斯FH模型能有效结合GVF的稳定性与数据驱动的自适应收缩，在理论和实证层面均优于传统方法，为小域估计中的方差建模提供了一种灵活且稳健的新工具。

Abstract: The Fay-Herriot (FH) model is widely used in official statistics to produce reliable estimates for domains with small sample sizes. In the classical FH model, the sampling variances are treated as known, even though they are typically estimated from the data. In practice, these variance estimates can be highly variable. To address this issue, practitioners often use Generalized Variance Functions (GVFs) to borrow strength across areas and stabilize estimation. In this work, we propose a new Bayesian model to improve the posterior estimation of the sampling variances in Small Area Estimation (SAE). Our proposed model incorporates Global-Local (GL) priors to improve the level of shrinkage toward the posterior estimates obtained with the GVF function. We study the theoretical properties of the proposed model and develop adaptive Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) algorithms to address computational challenges arising from conditional distributions involving Gamma functions. The performance of the proposed Bayesian model is investigated through simulation studies and compared with competing approaches. Finally, we implement our proposal in two real applications by estimating the Corn production from the U.S. Department of Agriculture and the Prevalence of the Educational Attainment Index at the municipality levels in Colombia.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [Context Graphs for Proactive Enterprise Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07721)
*Avinash Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于上下文图（Context Graph）的主动式企业AI系统，通过Delta检测引擎、Proactivity Scorer和LLM驱动的Surfacing Layer，在员工提问前主动推送相关信息，将平均信息呈现时间从47分钟降至30秒以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于RAG和智能体的企业AI系统本质上是反应式的——它们等待人类查询才行动。作者认为真正的企业生产力提升需要主动式智能体，即在员工提问之前就能主动呈现相关、可操作的信息。

Method: 提出三层架构：(1) Context Graph——基于NetworkX建模企业实体、关系及状态变化的活关系数据结构；(2) Delta Detection Engine——持续监测状态变化，Proactivity Scorer按紧迫性、相关性、角色匹配度对候选洞察排序；(3) Surfacing Layer——由Anthropic Claude API驱动的LLM，输出带依据解释的排序通知。同时推导了统一的Proactivity Score函数并提供了端到端的Python实现。

Result: 在三个通用企业案例（合同生命周期管理、工程事件响应、销售管道健康）中，Precision@5达0.83，误报率0.11，平均信息呈现时间从反应式基线的47分钟降至30秒以下。

Conclusion: 基于上下文图的主动式方法能显著提升企业信息系统的时效性和精准度，推动企业AI从被动响应向主动服务范式转变。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and agentic frameworks have advanced enterprise AI considerably, yet agents remain fundamentally reactive: they wait for a human query before acting. This paper argues that genuine enterprise productivity gains require proactive agents: systems that surface relevant, actionable information to workers before they ask. We propose the Context Graph, a live relational data structure that models enterprise entities, their relationships, and state transitions over time. Built on this graph, we define a Delta Detection Engine that continuously monitors state changes, a Proactivity Scorer that ranks candidate insights by urgency, relevance, and persona-fit, and a Surfacing Layer powered by an LLM that delivers ranked notifications with grounded explanations. We formalize each component, derive a unified Proactivity Score function, and provide a complete end-to-end Python implementation using NetworkX and the Anthropic Claude API. Evaluation across three generic enterprise case studies (contract lifecycle management, engineering incident response, and sales pipeline hygiene) demonstrates that context-graph-driven proactivity achieves Precision@5 of 0.83, a false positive rate of 0.11, and reduces mean time to surface from 47 minutes (reactive baseline) to under 30 second.

</details>


### [133] [AI-integrated models for assessing agricultural resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07759)
*Joshua R. Waite,Dana Golden,Brett Indelicato,Kevin Camp,Mojdeh Saadati,Shannon Regan,Patrick Schnable,Baskar Ganapathysubramanian,Carlos Messina,Suzanne Thornsbury,Soumik Sarkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文开发了一款AI驱动的工具，将经济模型GTAP与生物物理模型APSIM相结合，用于分析农业供应链冲击，并通过自然语言交互支持跨学科影响评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业供应链极易受到生物物理系统与经济系统耦合带来的连锁中断冲击，但现有分析工具往往将两类模型割裂，缺乏一种能够整合跨学科模型并以直观、低门槛方式进行交互式分析的手段。

Method: 论文提出并构建了一款AI驱动的工具，核心是将可计算一般均衡经济模型（GTAP）与农业生产系统模拟器（APSIM）进行集成。该工具支持用户以自然语言输入查询和接收响应，从而实现对供应链冲击的跨学科影响分析。

Result: （摘要中未明确给出具体的定量或定性结果，仅描述了工具的开发与功能。）

Conclusion: 通过将经济模型与生物物理模型融合，并以自然语言交互界面呈现，该工具有效赋能政策制定者与市场参与者，使其能够便捷地评估农业供应链冲击的跨学科影响。

Abstract: Agricultural supply chains are vulnerable to disruptions through linked biophysical and economic systems. We develop an AI-powered tool that integrates economic models (GTAP) with biophysical models (APSIM) to analyze supply chain shocks, enabling policymakers and market participants to assess cross-disciplinary impacts through queries and responses written in natural language.

</details>


### [134] [Adversarial Social Epistemology for Assemblies of Humans and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07760)
*Mihnea C. Moldoveanu,Joel A. C. Baum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"对抗性社会认识论"（ASE）框架，分析在密集交互的沟通环境中，行动者如何利用支撑公共断言可信度的承诺与权利机制进行信息操纵，并提供了审计和纠正信任违背的概念工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有概念（如认知气泡、回音室、错误信息扩散）不足以充分刻画一种更深层的现象：沟通行动者如何策略性地利用那些本应使"被支撑的断言"（scaffolded assertions）值得信赖的承诺结构（commitments）和权利结构（entitlements），通过扭曲、着色、省略、编造或策略性低度具体化信息来获取私利。这一空白需要新的分析框架来填补。

Method: 作者引入推理主义语义学（inferentialist semantics），将公共断言的意义定位于其在推理链中所承载的承诺与权利关系；在此基础上构建认知网络（epistemic networks）模型，刻画断言在证词、推理、制度认证和隐性信任链条中的传播结构；进而系统概述破坏支撑性公共传播信任的机制，以及通过审计推理链的可审计性来识别和纠正信任违背的机制。

Result: 论文提供了一套新的概念语言和分析机制，能够精确刻画行动者如何利用"支撑性断言"的承诺-权利结构进行策略性操纵，并给出了审计和纠正由此引发的信任违背的操作性框架。

Conclusion: 对抗性社会认识论为理解当代复杂沟通生态中的策略性信息操纵提供了比传统概念更精细的分析工具，揭示了信任被系统性颠覆的深层机制，并为修复认知生态中的信任结构指明了方向。

Abstract: We outline an adversarial social epistemology (ASE) for densely interactive communicative landscapes in which public assertions are scaffolded by chains of testimony, inference, institutional certification, and tacit trust. In such landscapes, agents have incentives and affordances to distort, color, omit, fabricate, or strategically under-specify information for private, reputational, rhetorical, or material gains. We argue that these phenomena are not adequately captured by familiar descriptions of epistemic bubbles, echo chambers, or misinformation diffusion. What requires explanation is how communicative agents exploit the commitments and entitlements that normally make scaffolded assertions trustworthy. We provide language that delivers the requisite analysis, outline mechanisms that subvert trust in scaffolded public communications, and outline machinery for auditing and redressing trust breaches arising from subverting the auditability of inferential chains, drawing on epistemic networks, enriched with an inferentialist semantics for interpreting assertions.

</details>


### [135] [Aligning Clinical Needs and AI Capabilities: A Survey on LLMs for Medical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07761)
*Qi Peng,Jiatong Li,Sirui Huang,Yiyang Jiang,Kaisong Gong,Ronger Ding,Shijie Ye,Changmeng Zheng,Yi Cai,Xiaobo Yang,Jin Huang,Xiao-Yong Wei,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了医学大语言模型在临床推理中的应用，提出双视角分析框架：临床视角基于Miller金字塔建立五层能力体系，计算视角将演绎、归纳、溯因推理模式与医学任务关联；同时构建五级医学推理基准数据集并评测18个前沿模型，揭示专科模型与通用模型在不同任务上的优劣势，最后探讨幻觉、数据等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在医疗领域展现出临床推理的潜力，但当前缺乏系统性的框架来连接临床实践需求与计算方法论，亟需统一的评估体系来衡量模型的医学推理能力层次。

Method: （1）提出双视角框架：临床侧基于Miller金字塔构建“知识回忆→动态病例管理”五级能力方案；计算侧将演绎、归纳、溯因三种推理模式映射到医学目标与任务。（2）构建覆盖五个医学推理能力层级的基准数据集，并评测18个前沿模型的表现。

Result: 评测结果表明：医学专科模型在以诊断为中心的任务上表现更优，而通用大模型在决策支持和对话任务上更具优势，揭示了不同模型类型在不同推理层级上的差异化能力。

Conclusion: 医学LLM在临床推理方面取得显著进展，但仍面临数据局限性、幻觉、知识接地等挑战；未来应致力于构建更安全、可靠、可融入临床工作流的系统。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as important tools in healthcare, showing growing potential for clinical reasoning and patient care. This survey examines recent progress in medical LLMs, focusing on reasoning applications and requirements. We present a dual-view approach that connects clinical practice with computational methods. On the clinical side, we establish a five-level competency scheme following Miller's Pyramid, progressing from knowledge recall to dynamic case management. On the computational side, we link deductive, inductive, and abductive reasoning patterns to common medical goals and tasks. We also introduce a benchmark dataset spanning five levels of medical reasoning capability and report results on 18 state-of-the-art models, revealing that medical specialist models excel in diagnosis-centric tasks while general models lead in decision support and dialogue. We conclude by discussing current progress and open challenges, including data limitations, hallucination, and grounding issues, and outline directions toward safer, more reliable, and workflow-ready systems.

</details>


### [136] [Alignment Plausibility: A New Standard for Assuring AI in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07766)
*Gwydion Williams,Sara Zannone,Bilal A Mateen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出，当前LLM在心理健康支持领域受注意力经济驱动，追求持续参与而非有效的治疗摩擦，导致依赖、边界侵蚀等慢性风险被忽视。作者主张仿照人类临床实践的三层安全保障（价值规范、训练内化、部署监督），构建"对齐可信性"（alignment plausibility）这一监管概念，以论证系统是否真正有利于患者健康结局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为心理健康支持提供者日益普及，但其设计受注意力经济逻辑支配，倾向于维持用户持续参与，而非提供有效心理支持所需的适当"摩擦"。当前安全应对多为被动反应，仅关注最显性的急性伤害，而依赖关系、边界侵蚀、扭曲信念放大等长期、隐蔽的风险未得到足够重视。

Method: 作者提出三层对齐框架，类比人类临床实践的安全保障机制：1）基于临床实践规范化的明确价值规范（explicit value specification）；2）将上述价值嵌入模型的训练（training）；3）在部署阶段检测价值漂移与长期伤害的监督机制（oversight），类似人类实践中的临床督导。这三层共同构成"对齐可信性"（alignment plausibility），并借鉴"生物可信性"（biological plausibility）的概念将其定位为AI在医疗健康领域的监管构造。

Result: 该框架产出了"对齐可信性"这一结构化论证工具，可系统性地论证系统价值、训练机制和监督机制是否共同指向安全且积极的健康结局。该概念被建议作为监管构造，用于支持或反对"系统已对齐到积极健康结果、不会造成伤害、最终使患者受益"这一信任判断。

Conclusion: 仅靠被动、零星的安全修补不足以应对LLM在心理健康领域的结构性风险。构建对齐可信性——即三层对齐路径的系统整合——为AI在健康领域提供了一种原则性的论证方式，有助于建立可信赖的、真正有利于患者的安全框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become significant providers of mental health support, yet they remain products of an attention economy whose operational and commercial targets favour sustained engagement over the friction that effective psychological support often requires. Developers' safety responses have been largely reactive, addressing the most visible and acute harms while subtler, longer-term patterns of risk (e.g., dependency, boundary erosion, the amplification of distorted beliefs) receive less attention. We contend that making LLMs structurally safe requires alignment organised at three levels that mirror how society assures the safety of human clinical practice: 1) explicit value specification grounded in the codified normative commitments of clinical practice; 2) training that embeds those values in the model; and 3) oversight that detects drift and longer-term harm during deployment, much as clinical supervision does for human practice. Organising alignment in this way yields a construct we call alignment plausibility - a structured demonstration that a system's values, training regime, and oversight mechanisms are together consistent with safe and positive outcomes. We propose alignment plausibility as a regulatory construct (by drawing analogy to the established construct of biological plausibility) for AI in health: a principled way to argue for, or against, trust that systems are aligned to positive health outcomes, will cause no harm even where capable of doing so, and will ultimately lead to patient benefit.

</details>


### [137] [Idiobionics: The Unification of Privacy and Intelligent Robotic Prostheses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07775)
*Kwesi Afari Darfoor,Patrick M. Pilarski,Bailey Kacsmar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"idiobionics"这一新的研究方向，系统性地探讨智能仿生义肢中的隐私安全问题。作者定义了该领域，梳理了相关文献，并提供了对抗性攻击的初步证据，同时列出了开放研究问题清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人假肢集成先进传感器和AI控制，它们在提升功能的同时也引入了可被恶意利用的威胁向量，威胁用户隐私。目前尚缺乏对这一隐私与智能仿生义肢交叉问题的系统性研究，若不解决这些隐私风险，可能阻碍用户对下一代仿生义肢的采纳。

Method: 作者采用概念定义与文献梳理相结合的方法：首先提出并定义了"idiobionics"这一新术语，将其扎根于可穿戴机器人、隐私安全和人机交互等相关文献中；然后通过初步实验演示了可能利用智能仿生义肢设计的对抗性攻击；最后整理了一份开放研究问题清单。

Result: 论文定义了idiobionics研究领域，展示了针对智能仿生义肢的潜在对抗性攻击的初步证据，并提供了一份面向可穿戴机器人及其他人机交互自主系统研究者的开放研究问题清单。

Conclusion: idiobionics研究有望帮助释放机器人假肢及相关仿生设备的全部潜力，呼吁学界正视并解决智能仿生义肢中的隐私挑战，以促进用户采纳和技术健康发展。

Abstract: The human body is at the center of a growing family of technologies designed to tightly and persistently couple biological and digital systems. Robotic prostheses are a representative example of this tight coupling. Also referred to as bionic limbs, robotic prostheses are devices that support people who have lost limbs in pursuing daily life activities such as walking and grasping objects. Bionic limbs are now perceptive and responsive owing to their integration with advanced sensors and artificial intelligence-based control approaches. Consequently, such robotic prostheses can now be viewed as semiautonomous wearable robotic systems that can co-adapt with their users. However, the same sensing and control advancements that increase the capability of robotic prostheses also introduce threat vectors that could be exploited by malicious entities to violate the privacy of users. To fully realize the benefits of next-generation bionic limbs, we maintain it is important to directly understand and address these privacy risks and the barriers they might present to user adoption. This paper therefore introduces a new line of inquiry we term idiobionics to holistically investigate issues at the intersection of privacy and intelligent bionic limbs. As the main contribution of this paper, we define idiobionics, ground it in related literature, and provide preliminary evidence showing and discussing potential adversarial attacks that could exploit intelligent bionic limb designs. We then contribute a curated list of open research questions within idiobionics that are relevant to researchers in wearable robotics and other human-facing autonomous systems. We expect that idiobionics research will help unlock the full potential of robotic prostheses and related bionic devices.

</details>


### [138] [Infinity-Parser2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07836)
*Zuming Huang,Jun Huang,Kexuan Ren,Baode Wang,Weizhen Li,Jianming Feng,Yu Wang,Yichen Yao,Shijun Lin,Yige Tang,Cheng Peng,Weidi Xu,Wei Chu,Yinghui Xu,Yuan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Infinity-Parser2 是一个基于可控数据合成流水线与多任务强化学习的大规模多模态模型，用于端到端文档解析。它开源了500万样本的中英双语语料库 Infinity-Doc2-5M，引入可验证的多任务奖励系统实现联合强化学习，并发布 Flash 和 Pro 两个版本，后者在 olmOCR-Bench 和 ParseBench 上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文档解析领域长期面临高质量标注数据稀缺的问题，且现有模型往往将感知、结构理解和推理割裂处理。本文旨在通过可控数据合成解决语料瓶颈，并通过多任务联合强化学习将多项文档理解任务统一在单一优化框架中。

Method: 首先，构建可扩展的合成引擎，将可控渲染框架与迭代精炼循环相结合，生成 500 万样本的双语语料库 Infinity-Doc2-5M，涵盖元素边界框、规范内容形式（Markdown、HTML、LaTeX、SMILES、结构化图表）和全页阅读顺序标注。其次，设计可验证的多任务奖励系统，对文档解析、布局分析、表格解析、数学公式解析、图表解析、化学式解析、文档 VQA 和通用多模态理解八项任务进行联合强化学习（Joint RL），统一感知、结构与推理的优化信号。最后，在同一架构下发布两个变体：Flash 版针对低延迟推理优化，Pro 版面向高精度场景。

Result: Infinity-Parser2-Flash 相比 Infinity-Parser-7B 实现了 3.68 倍的吞吐量提升。Infinity-Parser2-Pro 在 olmOCR-Bench 上达到 87.6%，在 ParseBench 上达到 74.3%，超越 DeepSeek-OCR-2、PaddleOCR-VL-1.5 和 MinerU2.5，并在图表、化学式和文档 VQA 上展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过可控数据合成与多任务强化学习的结合，Infinity-Parser2 有效缓解了文档解析标注数据稀缺的问题，并在多项基准上取得领先性能，为端到端文档解析提供了一套完整且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: We present Infinity-Parser2, a large multimodal model that couples a controllable data-synthesis pipeline with multi-task reinforcement learning for end-to-end document parsing, addressing the persistent scarcity of faithfully annotated parsing corpora. Our contributions are threefold. First, we build a scalable synthesis engine, pairing a controllable rendering framework with an iterative refinement loop, and use it to construct and open-source Infinity-Doc2-5M: a 5-million-sample bilingual (Chinese/English) corpus spanning diverse document types, annotated with element bounding boxes, canonical content forms (Markdown, HTML, LaTeX, SMILES, structured charts), and full-page reading order. Second, we introduce a verifiable, multi-task reward system that enables Joint Reinforcement Learning across eight co-trained objectives (document parsing, layout analysis, table parsing, math formula parsing, chart parsing, chemical formula parsing, document VQA, and general multimodal understanding), unifying perception, structure, and reasoning in a single optimization signal. Third, we release two variants under a shared architecture: Infinity-Parser2-Flash, optimized for low-latency inference with a $3.68\times$ throughput gain over Infinity-Parser-7B, and Infinity-Parser2-Pro, engineered for precision-critical settings. Infinity-Parser2-Pro reaches state-of-the-art 87.6% on olmOCR-Bench and 74.3% on ParseBench, surpassing DeepSeek-OCR-2, PaddleOCR-VL-1.5, and MinerU2.5, with strong generalization to charts, chemical formulas, and document VQA.

</details>


### [139] [VectorizationLLM: Smart Vectorization Based AI Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07846)
*Ryan Duke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VectorizationLLM 是一款基于 Google 开源大语言模型构建的专用教学助手，通过 RAG 知识库和系统提示架构，帮助学生在 MATLAB 中学习向量化、傅里叶分析等计算课程内容，强调概念解释而非直接给出答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工程计算教学中，学生常依赖直接获取答案而忽略对核心概念的理解。现有通用 LLM 缺乏针对特定课程内容的定制化引导能力，因此需要构建一个既能提供丰富示例又避免直接答题的专用教学助手，以提升学生在 MATLAB 中解决向量化、时/波向量分析、分段函数、傅里叶分析和微分方程等问题的自主学习能力。

Method: 基于 Google 开源权重 LLM 进行领域特化，构建 RAG（检索增强生成）知识库以涵盖课程讲义与示例；设计系统提示架构（system prompt）约束模型行为，使其仅提供概念解释、代码示例、文本说明和图像演示，而拒绝直接给出习题答案。

Result: 该模型能够以代码、文本和图像多种形式提供详细的概念解释和课堂示例，并成功部署于纽约理工学院电气与计算机工程系 CTEC 247 课程中，作为辅助教学工具使用。

Conclusion: VectorizationLLM 通过 RAG 增强与提示约束的有机结合，有效实现了“引导式教学”而非“答案投喂”的目标，为工程计算课程中的 LLM 教学应用提供了一种可参考的范式。

Abstract: VectorizationLLM is a specialized Large Language Model based on Google open-weight LLMs. The model is designed to assist students to learn smart vectorization, time/wave vector analysis, piecewise functions, Fourier analysis, and differential equations in MATLAB. The course application is CTEC 247: Applied Computational Analysis II by the Department of Electrical & Computer Engineering Technology at New York Institute of Technology Old Westbury. The LLM model is designed to be an instructive assistant, providing detailed explanations of concepts with examples from in-class notes without providing direct answers to questions. The model is designed with a RAG (Retrieval Augmented Generation) knowledge base and system prompt architecture. Examples in both code, text, and images are provided in the LLM responses.

</details>


### [140] [A Graph Neural Network Model for Real-Time Gesture Recognition Based on sEMG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07850)
*Pragatheeswaran Vipulanandan,Kamal Premaratne,Manohar Murthi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图网络的新型sEMG表征方法，利用图神经网络实现实时手势识别，在8名健康受试者中达到99%平均分类准确率，单次预测仅需48ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进手部假肢和增强现实应用需要精确、即时的手势识别，而现有基于前臂sEMG信号的方法在表征能力和实时性方面仍有提升空间。

Method: 提出一种基于图网络的新型sEMG表征方法，将前臂肌肉激活模式编码为图结构；基于此图网络，采用图神经网络（GNN）构建机器学习算法进行手势分类；使用Myoband（8电极环绕前臂）采集sEMG信号进行验证。

Result: 在8名健康受试者上，该方法达到99%的平均分类准确率，优于现有最先进技术；在M1 Pro CPU上，图构建与预测总耗时为48ms，满足实时性要求。

Conclusion: 基于图网络的sEMG表征结合图神经网络，在兼顾高精度（99%）与低延迟（48ms）的同时实现了实时手势识别，适用于假肢控制和增强现实等实际场景。

Abstract: For seemless control of advanced hand prostheses and augmented reality, accurate and immediate hand gestures recognition is essential. Surface electromyography (sEMG) signals obtained from the forearm are commonly employed for this purpose. In this paper, we present a novel approach for sEMG representation that utilizes graph networks which contain information about muscle activation patterns in the forearm. Based on these graph networks, we have developed a machine learning algorithm capable of real-time hand gesture recognition using a graph neural network. The algorithm's performance was evaluated using sEMG signals acquired from myoband, which has 8 electrodes placed around the forearm, involving 8 healthy subjects. The proposed method demonstrated an average classification accuracy of 99\%, surpassing the performance of state-of-the-art techniques. The average time for both graph construction and prediction stood at 48ms utilizing a M1 pro CPU, rendering the approach well-suited for real-time applications.

</details>


### [141] [Agentic AI and Retrieval-Augmented Models in Straight-Through Underwriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07858)
*Robert Richardson,Josh Meyers,Brian Hartman,David Sandberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了AI架构（从传统规则自动化到LLM、RAG及多智能体系统）如何支撑精算实践中的透明度、可审计性与人机协同治理，并通过一个面向小型商业企业主保单（BOP）的直通式核保智能体框架进行实证分析，结果表明多智能体RAG管线在多步骤和缺失信息场景中表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精算实践正面临非结构化文档、异构数据源与受监管决策流程带来的推理挑战；现有技术选择范围涵盖从规则自动化到LLM等多种架构，但亟需系统性地评估这些架构在透明度、可审计性和人机协同治理等精算核心需求下的适用性，尤其是直通式决策场景中的表现。

Method: 构建合成但逼真的实验环境，设计并对比三条核保管线：(i) 单一LLM基线；(ii) 朴素RAG系统；(iii) 多智能体"Agentic RAG"管线，后者融合了定向检索、第三方数据核验以及显式的多步骤规则评估。以小型商业BOP的直通式核保为测试任务进行评估。

Result: 多智能体Agentic RAG系统整体表现最优，尤其在多步骤推理和缺失信息场景中优势显著——结构化检索与反思机制有效帮助模型避免做出缺乏依据的直通式核保决策。

Conclusion: 多智能体架构通过结合定向检索、工具调用和反思循环，能够更好地满足精算领域对透明度、可审计性与稳健决策的严格要求，为AI在受监管精算工作流中的落地提供了有前景的技术路径。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is beginning to reshape actuarial practice, particularly in domains that require reasoning over unstructured documents, heterogeneous data sources, and regulated decision workflows. Actuaries now face a design space that ranges from traditional rule-based automation to large language models (LLMs), retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and multi-agent ``agentic'' systems that plan, retrieve, call tools, and reflect. This paper examines how these emerging architectures can support actuarial priorities such as transparency, auditability, and human-in-the-loop governance, with a focus on straight-through decision processes. To make these ideas concrete, we develop and analyze an agentic AI framework for straight-through underwriting of small commercial Business Owner Policies (BOPs). We construct a synthetic but realistic experimental environment and compare three underwriting pipelines: (i) a single-LLM baseline, (ii) a naive RAG system, and (iii) a multi-agent ``Agentic RAG'' pipeline that combines targeted retrieval, third-party data checks, and explicit multi-step rule evaluation. The agentic system performs best overall, with the largest gains in multi-step and missing-information scenarios, where structured retrieval and reflection help the model avoid unsupported straight-through decisions.

</details>


### [142] [Feedback Manipulation Regularization: Enabling Offline Agent Alignment for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07859)
*Benjamin Poole,Minwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了反馈操纵正则化（FMR），一种算法无关的方法，利用评估性反馈作为纠正信号，在单阶段离线训练中提升模仿学习策略的对齐性，在Safety Gymnasium测试中实现高达98%的失对齐减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法主要针对语言生成的上下文赌博机框架，采用多阶段流程结合人类示范与反馈，缺乏在完全序列决策环境中将这两种互补信号作为单一阶段离线训练的互联信号的研究。

Method: 提出反馈操纵正则化（Feedback Manipulation Regularization, FMR），一种算法无关的方法，将评估性反馈用作纠正信号，直接在单阶段离线训练中改进模仿学习策略的对齐表现。

Result: 在适配后的Safety Gymnasium环境中，FMR在多种模仿学习算法上显著提升了对齐能力，失对齐减少高达98%，且在数据稀缺（稀缺对齐示范、含噪声无信息示范）场景下依然鲁棒。

Conclusion: FMR证明了评估性反馈可作为有效的纠正信号，在完全序列决策环境中以单阶段离线方式显著改善模仿学习的对齐性，为解决AI对齐问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) research has increasingly shifted focus towards alignment, ensuring agents learn behaviors adhering to human values. While human demonstrations and feedback have proven crucial for alignment, existing approaches predominantly combine these signals using multi-stage pipelines designed for the contextual bandit framing of language generation. Yet little work explores how these complementary inputs can serve as a richer, interconnected signal for single-stage offline training in fully sequential decision-making environments. We propose Feedback Manipulation Regularization (FMR), an algorithm-agnostic method that harnesses evaluative feedback as a corrective signal to improve the alignment of imitation learning policies. We adapt Safety Gymnasium environments to be a principled testbed for alignment evaluation, demonstrating improved aptitude and up to a 98\% reduction in misalignment across a range of imitation learning algorithms. FMR remains robust in limited data regimes, even when learning from scarce aligned and uninformative noisy demonstrations.

</details>


### [143] [Nigeria Machinery: A Low-Resource Industrial Dataset with a Domain-Grounded Reasoning Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07883)
*Gospel Bassey,Vincent Fakiyesi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文发布了尼日利亚工业机械数据集（89条记录、28个指标，覆盖2006-2025年）及基于链式思维推理的提示-补全对（94条），旨在缓解非洲工业经济领域量化数据稀缺问题。同时，论文指出大语言模型构建数据集时常见"数值正确但领域脱钩"的问题，并通过改进方法将领域锚定率从1/78提升至94/94。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲经济体的工业机械数据稀缺，缺乏公开、可直接用于建模的数据集，导致定量分析和数值类语言模型训练难以开展。作者旨在填补这一空白，并提供可复用的数据构建方法论。

Method: 首先，发布"尼日利亚机械使用与故障数据集"，包含89条机器级记录、28个指标，覆盖制造业与油气行业（2006-2025），每条记录标注公开来源并有编码本解码。其次，提出一种从稀疏数值中构建链式思维（CoT）推理示例的方法，生成94条"提示-补全-推理轨迹"三元组，每条提示标注真实指标、子行业、年份和来源。最后，针对LLM构建数据集时"数值匹配但领域脱钩"的问题，通过领域锚定策略将领域相关提示比例从1/78提升至94/94。

Result: 产出两个核心成果：（1）尼日利亚机械使用与故障数据集（89条记录）；（2）94条CoT推理示例。领域锚定性显著提升：领域相关提示从先前版本的1/78升至94/94，且所有检索答案与源值完全匹配（84/84）。

Conclusion: 该数据集为种子/参考数据集而非大规模训练集（仅89条记录，17个指标仅含单一观测，多数推理行为检索而非多步计算），但为非洲工业经济量化分析提供了基础资源。作者以CC-BY-4.0许可发布数据、推理层及逐行溯源文件，并明确说明了局限性。

Abstract: There is relatively little, public, and model-ready data on industrial machinery for African economies. This makes it hard to do quantitative analysis or to train language models on numeric tasks grounded in that setting. We release two things to help with part of this problem. The first is the Nigeria Machinery Usage and Failures Dataset: 89 machine-level records across 28 indicators, covering Nigeria's manufacturing and oil and gas sectors from 2006 to 2025. Every record names a public source and is decoded by a codebook. The second is a method for building chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning examples from these sparse numeric values. The result is 94 prompt, completion, and reasoning-trace rows. In every row, the prompt names the real indicator, subsector, year, and source of the record it comes from. The data adaptation work was carried out by Adaption Labs. Along the way we describe a problem that is common when language models are used to build datasets. The prompts can match the real numbers while saying nothing about the real domain. We show that fixing this raises the share of domain-grounded prompts from 1 out of 78 in an earlier release to 94 out of 94, and that every retrieval answer now matches its source value (84 out of 84). We release the data, the reasoning layer, and a per-row provenance file under CC-BY-4.0. We are clear about the limits. With 89 records and 17 indicators that have only one observation, this is a reference and seed dataset, not a large training set. Most reasoning rows are retrieval rather than multi-step computation.

</details>


### [144] [Persona Cartography: Charting Language Model Personality Traits in Weight Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07916)
*Luke Baines,Anton Gonzalvez Hawthorne,Mariia Koroliuk,Irakli Shalibashvili,Clément Dumas,Konstantinos Voudouris,David Demitri Africa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM的"人格面具(persona)"建模为行为特质空间中的位置，借助OCEAN(大五人格)框架来量化、操控模型人格。通过训练低秩适配器来放大或抑制单一特质，发现适配器效应可单调缩放、近似线性组合，并在适度强度下保持能力基准表现。这些特质轴会影响下游安全行为(如神经质影响挫败感、宜人性影响谄媚倾向)。此外还引入无监督心理测量管道，从模型输出中恢复四个可解释的行为因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM表现出重复性的行为模式(人格面具)，这些模式影响泛化能力和安全性，但目前缺乏可靠工具来分解、测量和控制这些行为模式。需要一种系统化的方法来理解和操控模型人格。

Method: 以OCEAN(五大人格：开放性、尽责性、外向性、宜人性、神经质)为框架，训练低秩适配器(LoRA)来放大或抑制单一人格特质。通过三种方式评估效果：(1)经人类验证校准的LLM-judge；(2)特质特异性多选题基准；(3)标准能力基准。还引入无监督心理测量管道，从模型rollout中恢复行为因子。

Result: 在6个模型(4B-32B，三个家族)上验证：(1)每个适配器对目标特质的调节呈近似单调缩放；(2)多个适配器可近似加性组合以构建混合人格；(3)适度强度下保持能力基准性能；(4)特质轴影响下游安全行为——神经质调节影响挫败感，宜人性调节影响谄媚倾向；(5)无监督管道恢复出语气(tone)、主动性(initiative)、说教性(didacticism)、认知谨慎(epistemic caution)四个可解释因子。

Conclusion: 人格控制可被理解为在权重空间中学习、缩放和组合特质，这为连接人格测量、模型编辑和安全性研究搭建了桥梁。该框架提供了一种可操作的、细粒度的LLM行为调控方法。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit recurring behavioural patterns -- personas -- that shape generalisation and safety, but we lack reliable tools for decomposing, measuring, and controlling them. Our central insight is to treat personas as positions in a space of behavioural traits, using the OCEAN framework to describe model personas in terms of Openness, Conscientiousness, Extraversion, Agreeableness, and Neuroticism. We train low-rank adapters to amplify or suppress individual traits, and evaluate their effects using an LLM-judge calibrated against a human-validated panel, trait-specific multiple-choice benchmarks, and standard capability evaluations. Across six models from three families (4B-32B), we find that each adapter moves its target trait largely monotonically with scale, combines approximately additively with other adapters to construct mixed personas, and preserves performance on capability benchmarks at moderate scales. We further show that the induced trait axes affect safety-relevant behaviour in downstream evaluations: for example, moving along neuroticism and agreeableness axes affects frustration and sycophancy respectively. We also introduce an unsupervised psychometric pipeline that recovers four interpretable behavioural factors (tone, initiative, didacticism, epistemic caution) from model rollouts. Persona control can then be considered in terms of learning, scaling, and composing traits in weight space, providing a bridge between personality measurement, model editing, and safety.

</details>


### [145] [Evaluating the Effect of Frame Rate in Sequence-Based Classification of Autism-Related Self-Stimulatory Hand Idiosyncrasies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07957)
*Raunak Mondal,Peter Washington*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）的自动视频筛查，探讨了最优序列神经网络架构与时间采样率，以及小样本训练下的数据增强策略。LSTM和GRU在每15帧采样时分别达到97.5%和98.75%的最高准确率，远超CNN基线；数据增强消融实验表明水平翻转和上采样对性能至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球超过7500万ASD患者，但远程行为筛查的可扩展计算方法仍然匮乏。现有CNN方法在识别自闭症相关的自我刺激行为（如拍手、摇晃等）时准确率有限（62-76%），且小规模行为数据集上缺乏系统性的数据增强策略指导。

Method: 研究分为两个目标：(1) 在SSBD数据集上，以1、5、15、30、45、90帧的不同间隔提取姿态特征，训练LSTM和GRU序列模型，与CNN基线对比；(2) 在I3D迁移学习流程中应用十种数据增强策略进行消融实验，量化各技术的边际贡献；同时采用个性化机器学习方法，对每个受试者视频进行时间分割训练与测试。

Result: LSTM和GRU均显著优于CNN基线（62-76%），分别在15帧采样间隔达到97.5%和98.75%的峰值准确率。数据增强消融实验中，水平翻转单独使用准确率最高（48.78%），移除上采样导致性能下降最大，表明其在复杂行为视频增强中的必要性。个性化模型预测一致性良好（平均损失1.84，标准差0.79）。

Conclusion: 在数据稀缺的临床行为分类场景中，GRU/LSTM序列模型配合中等时间采样率（每15帧）表现最优，上采样和水平翻转是小样本行为视频训练中关键的数据增强策略。研究为实际部署提供了架构选择、采样率和增强策略的具体指导。

Abstract: Autism spectrum disorder (ASD) affects over 75 million individuals worldwide, yet scalable computational methods for remote behavioral screening remain limited. This study addresses two complementary challenges in automated detection of autism-related self-stimulatory behaviors from video: (1) identifying the optimal sequence-based neural network architecture and temporal sampling rate, and (2) characterizing data augmentation strategies for training on small behavioral datasets. For the first objective, long short-term memory (LSTM) and gated recurrent unit (GRU) models were trained on pose-derived features from the Self-Stimulatory Behavior Diagnosis (SSBD) dataset at frame sampling intervals of 1, 5, 15, 30, 45, and 90 frames. Both architectures exceeded prior convolutional neural network (CNN) baselines (62-76% accuracy), with peak accuracies of 97.5% (LSTM) and 98.75% (GRU) at a sampling interval of every 15 frames.
